國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國 10 月財政赤字大幅擴張、房市買氣探底，通膨壓力仍具韌性 
經濟 
⚫ 美國 2027 財年首月財政赤字達 4,040.6 億美元，年增 42.1%。當月總收
入 3,689.4 億美元（年減 8.8%），總支出 7,730 億美元（年增 12.2%），
截至 10 月底公債總額升至 40.52 兆美元，財政壓力顯著加劇。 
⚫ 受房貸利率攀升至 7.33%影響，2026 年 10 月美國成屋銷售年率跌至 375
萬戶的 16 年新低。目前房價中位數微升至 41.67 萬美元，待售庫存增
至 159 萬戶，房市呈現「量縮、價微揚、庫存增」的態勢。考量目前就
業市場仍持續成長，一旦利率開始回落，預期將能有效釋放市場積壓的
買氣。 
⚫ 10 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%，低於上月 0.6%，惟高於市場
預期；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%持平。住房、食品及能源價格續升，其中住
房貢獻逾三成，顯示通膨壓力仍存；惟受 2025 年 10 月政府停擺影響，
10 月年增率未發布。 
⚫ 10 月美國 PPI 月增 0.3%，較上月 1.4%明顯放緩，但年增率由 6.3%上
升至 6.5%，創 2022 年 11 月以來新高。能源與服務價格漲勢降溫，惟
食品、非能源商品及貨運價格續揚，顯示生產端通膨壓力仍高，後續須
關注成本轉嫁及對 Fed 利率政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 2026 年 APEC 峰會將於 11 月 18 日至 19 日在中國深圳舉行。會前中美
相繼延長部分關稅豁免及貿易限制暫停措施至 2027 年 1 月，中國暫停
24%對美加徵關稅，惟保留 10%稅率，美國則延長 178 項中國商品關稅
豁免，釋出經貿緩和訊號，有助降低供應鏈不確定性。惟雙方結構性分
歧仍存，後續須關注川習會談成果及貿易政策走向。 
⚫ 輝達創辦人黃仁勳因婚外情及涉國安爭議引發調查，於 11 月 12 日辭去
執行長及董事職務，結束 33 年掌舵生涯，由財務長克雷斯暫代執行長。
消息重挫輝達股價及市值，並引發市場對全球 AI 產業、供應鏈及公司
治理風險的高度關注。 
短評 
⚫ 美國 10 月財政赤字顯著擴大，反映歲入下滑及政府支出負擔加重；成
屋銷售創多年低點，顯示高利率持續壓抑房市需求。儘管 CPI 及 PPI 月
增幅放緩，惟核心通膨及生產端價格壓力仍頑強，顯示物價上行風險尚

經濟成長停滯，內外需動能轉弱

短評 ⚫ 經濟成長動能趨緩，內外需表現均偏弱，惟企業投資回升提供支撐，後 續景氣走勢仍取決於民間消費復甦及外需改善程度。 
中 
國 
國際能源價格上漲抑制貿易順差擴張 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月出口年增率較 9 月續增 1.6 個百分點至 37.9%，出口總值為 4,123
億美元；進口年增率增 10.6 個百分點至 41.6%，進口總值為 3,071 億美
元。 
⚫ 10 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 9 月的 5%升至 5.7%；但消費者通膨(CPI)、
核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別回落 0.2 個百分點為 0.7%、0.8%。 
短評 
⚫ 由於國際能源價格上漲，進口增速高於出口，並抑制貿易順差擴張、減
緩外匯存底流入速度，惟此亦有助於減緩人民幣升值速度。 
⚫ 在民間消費乏力之下，企業仍大多自行吸收成本，加上政府控制零售燃
油及民生水電價格漲幅，故目前原油價格高漲僅少數反映於消費端。

115.11.16 
一、 美國—10 月財政赤字大幅擴張、房市買氣探底，通膨壓力仍具韌性 
2026 年 10 月(2027 財年第 1 個月)聯邦政府財政赤字達 4,040.6 億美元，較去
年同期的 2,843.3 億美元大幅擴大 42.1% 。當月政府總收入為 3,689.4 億美元，年
減 8.8%，主因個人所得稅淨收入由去年同期的 2,169.5 億美元下滑至 1,863.8 億
美元，且 10 月關稅退稅高達 67.3 億美元(遠高於去年同期的 17.4 億美元)，大幅
抵銷了 259.5 億美元的關稅總收入，致使關稅淨收入雖較 9 月的 59.1 億美元回升
至 192.2 億美元，仍較去年同期的 313.5 億美元大減 38.7%。支出方面，10 月政
府總支出達 7,730.0 億美元，年增 12.2%，除因 11 月 1 日適逢非營業日，導致部
分現役與退役軍人薪資、退伍軍人福利及醫療保險等款項提前至 10 月撥付外，
醫療保險(1,700.5 億美元)、社會安全(1,426.9 億美元)及國防(1,288.8 億美元)等主
要項目均較去年同期成長；同時，10 月聯邦淨利息支出攀升至 1,030.8 億美元，
年增 13.4%。整體而言，在歲入下滑與福利、國防及利息支出擴張的雙重影響下，
美國新財年開局赤字顯著走高，截至 10 月底聯邦公債總額已進一步推升至 40.52
兆美元(其中公眾持有債務為 32.74 兆美元)，龐大債務規模與後續利息負擔仍將
是牽動美國財政穩健度的核心變數。 
2026 年 10 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率降至 375 萬戶，較上月減少
5.5%，較去年同期下滑 8.8%，創下自 2010 年 8 月以來的最低水準，主因是 30 年
期固定房貸平均利率攀升至 7.33%(高於上月的 6.86%及去年同期的 6.25%)，使購
屋負擔加重，全美住房可負擔指數亦由去年同期的 107.3 降至 100.7。觀察區域表
現，10 月西部、南部及中西部銷量分別月減 9.7%、6.5%及 3.2%，僅東北部維持
持平，且四大地區銷量均較去年同期衰退。儘管買氣受抑，全美成屋售價中位數
仍年增 0.4%至 41.67 萬美元，寫下連續 40 個月年增紀錄；供給端則持續擴增，
待售庫存升至 159 萬戶，月增 1.3%、年增 4.6%，相當於 5.1 個月供給量(高於上
月的 4.7 個月與去年同期的 4.4 個月)。全美房地產經紀人協會(NAR)首席經濟學
家 Lawrence Yun 指出，在高利率衝擊下，10 月成屋市場呈現「量縮、價微揚、
庫存增」的格局，但全國 8,800 萬名屋主整體財務狀況依然穩健，不良資產交易
僅占 2%，考量目前就業市場仍持續成長，未來一旦房貸利率回落，市場可望釋

出可觀的積壓買氣。 
2026 年 10 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.4%，低於上月之
0.6%，惟高於市場預期的 0.3%；扣除食品與能源價格之核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，與
前月持平，並高於市場預期的 0.2%。在細項方面，住房指數月增 0.3%，貢獻整
體 CPI 月增幅逾三成，仍為推升通膨的重要因素；食品指數月增 0.5%，其中家庭
食品及外食價格分別上漲 0.4%及 0.5%；能源指數月增 0.7%，其中汽油價格及電
價分別上漲 1.0%及 0.9%，惟汽油漲幅較上月明顯放緩。核心項目方面，休閒娛
樂、醫療照護、汽車保險、家庭用品及教育等價格持續上揚，二手車及卡車價格
則下跌 1.2%，通訊價格亦小幅下降。另受 2025 年 10 月美國政府停擺影響，當月
未進行 CPI 調查並缺乏比較基期資料，美國勞工局未發布 2026 年 10 月整體 CPI
及多數主要指數之年增率。整體而言，10 月美國物價仍受住房、食品及能源價格
上漲帶動，核心 CPI 月增率則連續第三個月維持 0.3%，顯示服務類通膨壓力仍具
韌性，後續通膨走勢仍須持續觀察能源價格及住房成本變化。 
2026 年 10 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 0.3%，明顯低於上
月修正後之 1.4%，惟年增率由 6.3%升至 6.5%，創 2022 年 11 月以來最大漲幅，
顯示生產端價格壓力仍高。其中，最終需求商品價格月增 0.5%，低於上月之 2.5%，
逾六成漲幅來自不含食品及能源之商品價格上升，其中基本有機化學品價格上漲
3.9%；食品價格亦月增 1.3%，惟能源價格微降 0.1%，其中柴油價格下跌 7.9%。
另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.2%，低於上月之 1.0%，主要受運輸及倉儲
服務價格上漲 2.0%帶動，其中貨運卡車運輸價格上漲 3.9%，貢獻服務價格漲幅
逾八成，惟貿易服務價格下降 0.2%，抵銷部分漲幅。剔除食品、能源及貿易服務
後之核心最終需求 PPI 月增 0.5%，與上月持平，年增率則由 5.0%小幅降至 4.9%。
整體而言，10 月美國 PPI 月增幅明顯放緩，主要反映能源價格回落及服務價格漲
勢降溫，惟食品、部分非能源商品及貨運價格仍持續上漲，加上整體 PPI 年增率
進一步攀升，顯示生產端通膨壓力仍高，後續須關注企業成本轉嫁情形、能源及
運輸價格變化，以及對消費者物價與聯準會利率政策之影響。 
2026 年亞太經濟合作組織(APEC)領袖峰會將於 11 月 18 日至 19 日在中國深
圳舉行。在峰會召開前及「川習三會」前夕，中美雙方相繼延長部分關稅豁免與
暫停措施，釋出緩和貿易緊張關係的訊號，並為後續經貿協商創造較有利條件。
中國宣布自 11 月 10 日起續暫停實施 24%的對美加徵關稅兩個月，並保留 10%稅

率作為後續談判籌碼，同時延長對美船舶特別港務費、出口管制及部分反制措施
的暫停期限；美國則將 178 項中國商品的 301 關稅豁免延長至 2027 年 1 月 9 日，
涵蓋工業產品、太陽能設備及電子零組件等項目，並同步延後對中國船舶港口費
及岸橋設備加徵關稅措施。此番互釋善意反映雙方均有意維持經貿關係穩定，降
低企業經營及供應鏈不確定性，並為 APEC 期間的高層對話創造有利條件；惟中
方仍保留部分關稅措施，顯示未完全放棄反制工具，後續發展仍將視雙方談判進
展而定。 
輝達(NVIDIA)創辦人黃仁勳近日在被爆出與疑涉中國情報背景的女子黃樂
(Alice Huang)有婚外情並於 11 月 8 日坦承對妻子不忠致歉後，因外部與董事會調
查持續延燒，且《紐約時報》與《路透社》接連揭露其與直屬下屬輝達醫療保健
副總裁金伯莉·鮑爾(Kimberly Powell)及另一名內部女員工也存在婚外情，最終於
11 月 12 日正式宣布辭去總裁、執行長與董事會職位，結束其掌舵輝達 33 年的傳
奇生涯，並由財務長克雷斯(Colette Kress)暫代執行長兵符，同時任命哈維·瓊斯
(Harvey C. Jones)為首任董事會主席。同時，美國總統川普於 11 月 9 日證實聯邦
調查局(FBI)已介入調查該起事件及潛在的反情報國安疑慮，並表示雖與黃仁勳通
話且相信其不知女方背景的說法，仍會交由情報部門進一步核實。黃仁勳閃辭的
消息隨即震撼全球科技與金融市場，導致輝達股價在 11 月 12 日開盤後盤中一度
重挫逾 14%、單日市值一度蒸發約 6,000 億美元，不僅重創高「含輝量」台股 ETF
表現，更引發外界對全球 AI 產業版圖及美中科技供應鏈穩定性的高度憂慮。儘
管黃仁勳矽谷盟友與部分員工近期發起連署與遊說，試圖推動他重返輝達，但美
國財政部長貝森特(Scott Bessent)已公開呼籲輝達領導層應進行「大幅改組」，使
後續接班與公司治理動向備受矚目。 
二、歐元區—勞動市場開始出現疲態 
 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率二次估計值維持 0.4%不變，顯示在地緣政治不確
定性升高、能源價格再度上揚的環境下，整體景氣仍具支撐力；今年前三季經濟
成長率(年化率)則大約為 1.1%，大致持平去年水準。從需求面來看，政府支出與
民間消費為主要成長動能，其中民間消費受惠於較高的儲蓄水準及穩定的就業市
場，減輕能源價格上漲的衝擊，並且電動車、油電混合車需求持續增加，抵銷利
率升高及地緣政治波動對投資帶來的負面影響。

### Page 8

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

7

展望未來，從歐元區採購經理人指標(PMI)顯示第四季經濟仍可望延續溫和擴
張，在能源衝擊逐漸消退後，2027 年成長動能有望進一步增強。不過，第三季就
業呈現零成長，主要是德、法等核心國家就業疲弱，抵銷南歐國家就業增加的影
響，勞動市場可能逐漸走疲為未來復甦的隱憂。若就業狀況不如預期，恐進一步
抑制薪資表現，並削弱消費信心，進而影響經濟復甦。 
三、日本—經濟成長停滯，內外需動能轉弱 
 2026 年第三季經濟成長季增率持平，結束連續三季的經濟擴張，主要受通膨、
部分地區自然災害及全球需求疲弱影響，導致私人消費季減 0.3%，出口亦下滑
0.2%，加上進口反彈 2.5%，使淨貿易拖累 GDP 達 0.6 個百分點；惟企業投資季
增 1.6%，為近三季以來首次成長，對景氣形成一定支撐。整體而言，日本內外需
動能均有所轉弱，後續民間消費回溫及企業投資能否持續，將是觀察經濟能否恢
復成長的重要指標。 
四、中國—國際能源價格上漲抑制貿易順差擴張 
10 月出口年增率較 9 月續增 1.6 個百分點至 37.9%，出口總值為 4,123 億美
元；累計前十個月出口年增率上升為 22.7%，累積出口總值為 37,827 億美元。前
十個月出口主要成長項目(累積年增率)包含 IC(111.1%)、自動數據處理設備
(60.6%)、船舶(27.7%)、汽車(52.2%)，其中 IC 及自動數據處理設備出口加速成長，
為 10 月出口年增率加速上升的主要因素。 
10 月進口年增率較 9 月增 10.6 個百分點至 41.6%，進口總值為 3,071 億美
元；累計前十個月進口值成長 29%至 27,401 億美元。該月原油、成品油進口金額
大幅增加，為帶動進口加速成長的主要因素；IC、自動數據處理設備(包含伺服器
等)進口分別成長 65.1%、136.7%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部分伺服器
關鍵組件有賴於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前十個月貿易順差 10,426 億美
元，僅較去年同期增加 8.7%，增幅明顯低於出口，顯示出口擴張同時伴隨關鍵原
物料進口大幅增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價值產品依賴
國際供應鏈支持。此外，因貿易順差增幅有限，10 月外匯存底僅增 49 億美元至
約 3.41 兆美元。 
10 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 9 月的 5%升至 5.7%，主因原油價格居高不下，
燃料購進成本持續上漲，提高生產成本所致。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格

### Page 9

用相關內容。

8

年增率續揚 10.5 個百分點至 35%，並帶動燃料加工業出廠價格年增率上揚至
29.9%，此外，對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學品、化纖出廠價格年增率加速升
至 16.4%、16%，顯示原油價格上漲的影響開始擴散至中下游產業。此外，由於
近期鋼鐵價格走弱，黑色金屬礦產開採出廠價格年減 6.4%，為主要拖累因素。 
10 月消費者通膨(CPI)、核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別回落 0.2 個百分點為
0.7%、0.8%。儘管燃油通膨續增 5.3 個百分點至 20.2%，但食品、房租、汽車、
旅遊價格漲幅皆有放緩，其他商品及服務價格則大致平穩，使消費者通膨較前月
下滑。整體而言，雖然企業生產成本持續上升，但在民間消費乏力之下，企業仍
大多自行吸收成本，加上政府控制零售燃油及民生水電價格漲幅，故目前原油價
格高漲僅少數反映於消費端。惟考量能源成本傳導存在時間差，若油價維持高檔
或進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。 
五、印度及東南亞—印度通膨超標；印尼、菲律賓第三季 GDP 分別達 5.5%及
2.1% 
(1)印度：10 月消費者物價指數（CPI）年增率進一步上揚至 6.09%，遠高於 9 月
的 5.54%，其中食品通膨加速上升至 8.5%，反映聖嬰現象導致季風降雨量低於預
期，影響農作物收成，同時核心通膨率（剔除汽油、柴油和貴金屬）從 9 月的 3.2%
上升至 3.8%，顯示潛在通膨持續走強並有所擴大。由於通膨率高於預期且突破央
行 6%的目標區間上限，預期印度央行 12 月的政策會議將有進一步升息的壓力。 
(2)印尼：2026 年第三季經濟成長率(GDP)年增 5.47%，私人消費成長 5.04%，主
要因政府採取措施，協助家庭抵禦通膨壓力和更高的借貸成本，惟受能源補貼負
擔加重導致財政空間收窄，政府支出大幅放緩至 8.95%，遠低於上季的 15.97%；
固定資產投資成長略放緩至 6.06%；另受石油和能源成本上漲影響，進口成長率
（7.31%）高於出口成長率（4.59%），致淨出口產生負貢獻，惟較上季對經濟的
拖累有所減緩。按產業別觀之，製造業(5.87%)、住宿業（11.22%）、運輸/倉儲業
（8.03%）和通訊業（7.13%）均有所成長，採礦業產出由負轉正 0.9%，抵銷農業
產出受聖嬰現象影響而大幅下降至 0.5%的影響。整體而言，第三季經濟成長優於
預期，惟在財政和貨幣政策收緊的背景下，國內需求可能進一步放緩。 
(3)菲律賓：2026 年第三季經濟成長率(GDP)年增 2.1%，為 2009 年第四季以來（不
計新冠疫情影響）最疲軟的經濟成長，低於政府先前已下調的全年 3.5%至 4.5%

用相關內容。

9

的目標，聖嬰現象令農業產量低迷和能源成本高漲持續抑制經濟活動。家庭消費
成長放緩至 2.4%，政府支出成長降至 6.8%，固定投資雖小幅復甦仍萎縮 4.4%，
所幸出口成長 8.6%、進口成長放緩至 7.7%，致淨出口呈正向貢獻。按產業別觀
之，工業成長加快至 5.4%，服務業小幅放緩至 9%，惟農業產出受氣候影響而衰
退 0.4%。整體而言，第三季經濟活動主要受氣候干擾，農業、服務產出下滑並影
響消費支出，固定投資在公共建設投資帶動下有好轉跡象，然而央行升息提高借
貸成本，對私人投資產構成壓力，隨極端天氣事件的影響逐漸消退、家庭在年底
假期前消費意願提升、以及政府加快基礎設施建設，將有助於第四季經濟加快復
甦。 
六、台灣—AI 商機持續發酵，出口動能強勁 
10 月出口達 967.6 億美元，年增 58.9%，創歷史新高，主要由 AI 及高效能運
算需求帶動，其中資通與視聽產品、電子零組件合計約占總出口 83%，顯示 AI 伺
服器、半導體及相關零組件已成為出口成長核心。同時，對美出口達 329.3 億美
元、年增 55.9%，東協亦大幅成長 83.8%，反映 AI 需求及全球供應鏈重組正帶動
出口市場結構改變。惟進口年增 75.1%，增幅高於出口，後續宜觀察進口增加是
否有效轉化為產能及出口，以及 AI 需求、高基期與全球科技業資本支出變化對
出口動能的影響。

予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が 6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比 9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。

Document 106: 2027年春闘の初動モメンタム・アップデート ～経労委報告原案と産別要求からも賃上げ意欲を確認～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027年春闘の初動モメンタム・アップデート ～経労委報告原案と産別要求からも賃上げ意欲を確認～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年 11 月 2 日

1/3 
2027 年春闘の初動モメンタム・アップデート 
～経労委報告原案と産別要求からも賃上げ意欲を確認～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 経団連による、2027 年春闘に向けての経労委報告原案が判明した。高い賃上げの「社会全体
への浸透」を掲げており、大企業を中心に高い賃上げが定着しつつあるなか、その動きを中
小企業などにも広げることに重点を置く姿勢がうかがえる。また、筒井経団連会長の発言か
らは、ベア実施の検討をスタンダードとする従来の方針が維持されていることも確認できる。 
○ 流通やサービス、繊維などの労働組合で構成されるＵＡゼンセンも定昇込み６％、ベア４％
など、前年並みの高い賃上げ要求を掲げた。労使とも、賃上げに向けての姿勢にトーンダウ
ンはみられない。 
○ 一方、賃上げ率のさらなる加速を示唆する材料は現時点で多くない。連合の要求目安は前年
より強まったものの、経団連は賃上げ率の加速までは示していないとみられ、ＵＡゼンセン
の要求率は前年と同水準である。2027 年春闘では、５％近傍という高い賃上げ率を維持でき
るかが焦点となる。 
○ 日銀の植田総裁は、2027 年春闘で２％のインフレ率を前提とした賃金設定が行われることの
重要性を指摘した。2026 年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍化するなかでも高い賃上げが続くかど
うかは、２％の物価安定目標の定着や今後の金融政策を判断するうえで重要となる。

経労委報告原案は、高い賃上げの「社会全体への浸透」を掲げる 
10 月22 日のレポート（2027 年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも
昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～ | 新家 義貴 | 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所）では、連
合の基本構想や経団連の経労委報告骨子案などをもとに、2027 年春闘に向けた賃上げ姿勢は労使
双方とも前年からのトーンダウンはみられないと評価したが、その後、経団連の経労委報告原案
やＵＡゼンセンの要求方針素案が明らかになった。本稿では、これらの新たな材料を踏まえ、春
闘の初動のモメンタムをアップデートする。 
10 月30 日、経団連が 2027 年春闘に向けて取りまとめる「経営労働政策特別委員会報告（経労
委報告）」の原案内容が報道された。報道によれば、原案では賃上げの力強いモメンタムを継続

### Page 2

2/3 
し、その動きを社会全体に浸透させるために取り組みを強化する方針が示された。特に、中小企
業が継続的・計画的な賃上げを実施できるかが重要とされ、適正な価格転嫁に加え、大企業が持
つ人材や技術を活用して中小企業の収益力向上を支援する必要性が指摘された。また、ＡＩの普
及を踏まえ、働き手が生み出した成果をどのように評価し、賃金などの処遇へ反映するかについ
て、見直しを求めている。生産性向上によって生み出された付加価値を賃金にどう配分するか
は、今後の労使交渉においても重要な課題となるだろう。 
経団連はこれまで、2023 年を賃上げの「起点」、2024 年を「加速」、2025 年を「定着」、2026 年
を「さらなる定着」の年と位置付けてきた。今回の原案では「社会全体への浸透」が掲げられて
おり、大企業を中心に高い賃上げが定着しつつあるなか、その動きを中小企業などにも広げるこ
とに重点を置く姿勢がうかがえる。2026 年春闘向けの経労委報告でも、中小企業における構造的
な賃上げや適正な価格転嫁の重要性が強調されていたが、今回の原案では、大企業による人材・
技術面での支援にも言及するなど、賃上げの裾野を広げるための取り組みをより具体的に示して
いる。 
賃上げに対する経営側の基本姿勢も維持されている。2026 年春闘向けの経労委報告では、ベア
実施の検討を賃金交渉のスタンダードと位置付けていたが、筒井経団連会長は今年９月７日の記
者会見でも、「ベースアップ実施の検討を引き続きスタンダードと位置付けていきたい」と述べて
いる。ベアに対する従来の方針が維持されていることは重要だ。今春以降のイラン情勢悪化を受
け、原材料費や物流費の上昇によって企業の賃上げ余力が圧迫されることが懸念されていたが、
少なくとも現時点で経団連が賃上げに慎重な姿勢へ転じた様子はうかがえない。

UAゼンセンも前年並みの高い要求を維持 
労働側でも、主要産業別労働組合組織の要求方針が明らかになり始めた。10 月 27 日、流通やサ
ービス、繊維などの労働組合で構成されるＵＡゼンセンは、2027 年春闘の要求方針素案を公表し
た。正社員組合員については、定昇込みで６％、ベア等の賃上げ分で４％を基本とし、賃金水準
が最低基準に満たない組合などでは格差是正分を加えて７％の賃上げを要求する。また、短時間
組合員については、制度昇給分に加えて５％、制度昇給分が明確でない場合には７％を目安とす
るとのことである。 
これらの要求水準は、いずれも 2026 年春闘から変わっていない。６～７％という要求水準は依
然として高いが、要求率を前年からさらに引き上げたわけではないことには注意が必要だ。連合
は 2027 年春闘に向けた基本構想で「６％中心、５％以上」と前年より強い要求目安を示したが、
実際には前年の段階で、すでに６％程度の賃上げを要求していた産別組織もある。今回の連合の
方針には、こうした既存の要求水準を追認する側面もあり、ＵＡゼンセンの要求方針はその一例
といえる。

前年からのトーンダウンはみられていないが、加速しているわけでもない  
以上を踏まえると、2027 年春闘の初動のモメンタムは引き続き良好と評価できる。経団連はベ

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
ア実施の検討をスタンダードとする従来の方針を維持し、高い賃上げを中小企業などにも広げる
ことで「社会全体への浸透」を目指す姿勢を示している。労働側でも、連合が前年より強い要求
目安を掲げており、ＵＡゼンセンも前年並みの高い要求水準を維持した。イラン情勢悪化に伴う
コスト増加が懸念されるなかでも、労使双方の賃上げ姿勢に前年からのトーンダウンはみられな
い。 
もっとも、賃上げ姿勢が慎重化していないことと、賃上げ率がさらに高まることは別問題であ
る。経団連が重視しているのは高い賃上げの継続とその裾野の拡大であり、賃上げ率の加速を示
唆しているわけではないだろう。また、ＵＡゼンセンの賃上げ要求は高いが、要求率自体は前年
から据え置かれている。連合の要求目安は強まったものの、現時点で賃上げ率の加速を見込むだ
けの材料は揃っていない。エコノミストの予想を集計するＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査（10 月調
査）でも、2027 年春闘の賃上げ率予想は4.96％とされており、前年実績の 5.18％から小幅に鈍化
するとの見方となっている（数値は厚生労働省ベース）。 
当面の焦点は、５％近傍という高い賃上げ率を維持できるかどうかにある。もっとも、今後の
経済・物価情勢によっては企業の賃上げ余力や労働側の要求姿勢も変化し得るため、先行きを慎
重に見極める必要がある。当面、自動車総連や電機連合など主要産業別労働組合組織の要求方針
が前年からどの程度変化するかが注目される。 
金融政策の観点からも2027 年春闘の動向は重要である。日銀の植田総裁は 10 月 30 日の記者会
見で、基調的な物価上昇率が２％の物価安定の目標と概ね整合的な水準に達したとの認識を示し
た上で、2027 年春闘について、労使交渉でも２％のインフレ率が前提として織り込まれることが
必要との考えを示した。一方、2026 年の物価上昇率が過去２～３年より低くなる可能性を指摘
し、それが春闘の結果にどう影響するかを注視する姿勢も示している。 
ここ数年の春闘では、物価上昇に負けない賃上げを実現することが、高い賃上げ要求を支える
重要な要因となってきた。2026 年の物価上昇率は 2025 年と比べれば鈍化する見込みであり、こう
した観点からの賃上げ圧力はやや弱まる可能性がある。 
もっとも、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを前提として賃金を設定するよう
になれば、足元の物価上昇率が低下しても安定的な賃上げは持続しやすくなる。日銀は基調的な
物価上昇率が２％に「到達」したと判断したが、２％の物価安定目標が「定着」したとまでは判
断していない。その意味でも、2027 年春闘は、２％の物価上昇を前提とした賃金設定がどの程度
定着しているかを確認する機会となる。春闘に向けた労使双方の姿勢や実際の賃上げ動向は、今
後の日銀の金融政策判断においても重要な材料となるだろう。

参考文献 
2027 年春闘のスケジュールと金融政策 ～2027 年春闘の行方は3 月より前に見えてくる～ | 新家 
義貴 | 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所

みられず～ | 新家 義貴 | 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 November 2026 Citi Research
3
CPO: New growth opportunities from vertical integration — Hon Hai sees
significant long-term opportunities in both ASIC and vertical integration including
optical interconnect technologies. In optical networking, management believes the
transition from electrical to optical interconnects is structurally inevitable as AI
models expand and memory-bandwidth requirements increase. Hon Hai’s CPO

optical switching already starts mass volume production for its major customer,
and its micron-level precision manufacturing expertise in optical alignment,
connectors, and electrical-to-optical conversion could create additional value
through component integration and higher system-level content. Its extensive
engineering resources, ODM/JDM development capabilities, and vertically
integrated manufacturing platform should strengthen its competitive positioning.

Management expressed strong confidence in optical growth opportunities but did
not provide specific 2027 revenue guidance.

Robust financials and earnings growth, maintain Buy — Thanks to strong
demand from major CSP clients and its continuous automation and vertical
integration development, Hon Hai’s capex increased 43% YTD. Nevertheless, its
EBITDA increased 50.3% YoY to NT$369.2bn. To improve capital efficiency, Hon

Hai plans to expand consignment arrangements, optimize inventory and
receivables management, and carefully evaluate investment payback periods.
Management reiterated its long-term operating margin floor of above 3%, with
further potential upside from scale economies, automation, product mix
improvements, and greater vertical integration.
Given Hon Hai’s solid result and better operational leverage, we raise our
2026/2027/2028 EPS by 4%/5%/5%. Considering its solid execution, stable

dividend yield and attractive valuation, we reiterate our Buy rating with TP NT$400
unchanged. Our TP implies 15x of our 2027 EPS projection.

### Page 9

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 November 2026 Citi Research
9
Hon Hai Precision
Company description
Established in 1974, Hon Hai has become the largest EMS player in the world
in terms of sales. Through its vertical integration model or "eCMMS" (e-
enabled Components, Modules, Moves and Services), it provides
components, modules and system assembly products to many global OEMs
in the PC, communication and consumer sectors.
Investment strategy
We rate Hon Hai shares as Buy as we believe the company’s margins will

continue to improve, backed by a renewed focus on profitability, operational
efficiency and new businesses development. We think there’s more upside to
go from current level, as the underlying drivers are not just about product
cycle, but also about fundamental transformation and new business
opportunities.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 15x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is
at the higher end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is

justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook,
as well as new endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon
Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe
a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

activities and services intended to benefit the investor clients of Citigroup Global Markets Inc. and its affiliates (the “Firm”).

principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between
institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to
participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Our recent downgrades have generated investor pushback. We
acknowledge that demand remains strong, but believe exposure
should be reduced before cloud-capex deceleration becomes

visible.
Following Cloud Semis: Peak Growth, Less Upside  and Old Memory: Peak Pricing
Power?, we address the main investor questions below.
1. Are the cuts to Montage’s earnings forecasts justified?
We acknowledge that our revised 2027 RCD shipment assumptions may prove too
conservative. We would characterize the shipment and revenue revisions as
cautious, incorporating uncertainty around procurement timing, platform transitions
and supply-chain execution. These assumptions leave room for upside if customer

orders and deployment proceed more smoothly than we currently allow for.
Structural content growth remains supportive, and sustained order strength would
warrant revisiting the degree of conservatism in our estimates.
2. What justifies the multiple compression (across memory and cloud semis),
and where is the catalyst?
Our central consideration is to reduce exposure before the cloud-capex deceleration
becomes visible. Valuations can adjust as investors reassess the durability of

growth, even while earnings continue to rise. Peaking earnings-revision breadth and
weaker visibility into 2028 server production reduce our confidence in extrapolating
recent growth rates.
For old memory, the concern is also around the sustainability of pricing and returns
as shortages narrow. Limited fixed-price LTAs enabled rapid price increases during
the upcycle, but provide less protection when demand weakens. Our updated
supply-demand models point toward normalization into 2028, supporting lower

valuation multiples before peak profitability rolls over.
The potential catalysts are softer order momentum and slower price increases. Such
timing remains uncertain, but we believe waiting for these developments to become
fully visible risks missing the valuation adjustment. Faster deployment, renewed
earnings upgrades and sustained capex acceleration beyond 1Q27 would challenge
our cautious view. (Continued)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
November 15, 2026 03:36 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
 Idea
2

3. Have we changed our server or CPU shipment forecasts? If demand remains strong, 
why act now?
We have made no such industry-wide revisions. The hyperscalers’ 3Q results confirmed 
strong current spending. However, following the US midterms, our US team shifted 
approximately 5GW of expected US datacenter capacity additions from 2028 into 2029, 
equivalent to around 20% of previously expected 2028 additions (see Buy the Drop: AI 
Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms). Our US team's hyperscaler capex 
forecasts remained unchanged at US$1.6trn and US$1.8trn for 2027 and 2028. This 
creates greater uncertainty around the timing of equipment procurement and deployment 
even with spending intentions intact.
We believe the combination of the completed 3Q earnings season and greater post-
election execution uncertainty provides a reasonable point to incorporate more risk. 
Current demand can remain strong while the balance of potential earnings revisions and 
valuation outcomes becomes less attractive.
4. Why retain Overweight on CXMT and Nanya Tech?
We differentiate by product mix, technology migration and valuation. Niche memory has a 
smaller supply base, more volatile pricing and generally less direct exposure to AI 
demand. Its pricing can respond sharply to relatively modest changes in supply, customer 
inventories and consumer demand. These characteristics make recent scarcity-driven 
profitability more vulnerable as supply catches up.
CXMT and Nanya also face memory-cycle risk. However, CXMT has additional growth 
drivers from capacity expansion, localization and process tech migration. We also continue 
to see tight DDR5 supply, with CXMT management indicating that customers had reserved 
2027 capacity for relevant enterprise products. For Nanya, stronger process technology 
and a clearer DDR5 migration path differentiate it from other DDR4 suppliers in our 
coverage, with DDR5 already contributing approximately 10% of its revenue.
Aspeed and Montage have concentrated exposure to server deployment and the cloud 
investment cycle. Their content-growth opportunities remain intact, but their earnings 
trajectories and valuations are particularly sensitive to changes in deployment timing and 
expectations for cloud-spending growth. We therefore see a stronger case for reducing 
exposure ahead of visible deceleration.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

優群 3Q26 獲利低於預期，主要受筆電需求疲弱、稼動率下降及原
物料成本上漲影響，預期 4Q26 營運仍將承壓。惟隨 Google 伺服
器新案及網通新產品於 2027 年放量，加上高單價新產品陸續導
入，營收與獲利可望重返成長。研究部下修優群 2026、2027 年稅
後 EPS 至 10.06 元（原估 11.31 元）及 11.83 元（原估 14.30 
元）。考量短期利空已逐步反映於股價，現價對應 2027 年本益比
僅 8.8 倍，低於歷史區間下緣，且預估殖利率約 7.6%，具備下檔
支撐及評價修復空間。研究部以 2027 年 EPS 為評價基礎，給予 
12 倍本益比，目標價 140 元，投資評等由中立調升至買進。

重點摘要： 
1. 筆電需求疲弱、稼動率下降，3Q26 獲利低於預期 
優群 3Q26 營收 9.75 億元（+2.9% QoQ、−13.8% YoY），低於原
估 10.90 億元，主因筆電客戶持續調整出貨，SO-DIMM 與 M.2 營
收分別年減 36% 及 20%，抵銷伺服器與新產品成長貢獻。受營收
不如預期、產能利用率下降及原物料成本上漲影響，毛利率降至 
46.1%（−1.5ppt QoQ、−5.1ppt YoY），低於原估 47.4%；營益率
降至 27.8%，低於原估 29.9%。稅後淨利 2.55 億元（+18.9% QoQ、
−32.8% YoY），稅後 EPS 2.84 元，低於原估 3.42 元。

表一、優群 3Q26 獲利檢討及 4Q26 獲利修正

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 筆電需求持續疲弱，下修 4Q26 獲利預估 
優群 10 月營收 2.58 億元（−22.9% MoM、−25.9% YoY），主要受
筆電客戶持續調整出貨影響。預期 11、12 月出貨將較 10 月回
升，惟整體需求仍偏弱，產能利用率下降將持續壓抑毛利率表現。
考量筆電需求修正幅度超出預期，研究部下修 4Q26 營收預估至 
8.80 億元（−9.8% QoQ、−15.5% YoY，原估 10.26 億元），毛利
率由原估 47.8% 下修至 45.4%，稅後 EPS 由 2.69 元下修至 
2.02 元（−29.3% QoQ、−37.9% YoY）。

3. CSP 伺服器與網通新產品放量，2027 年營運結構持續優化 
隨 AI 推論及企業運算需求成長，伺服器對記憶體容量、頻寬及傳
輸效能的要求持續提升，帶動 DDR5 R-DIMM 朝高速化與高容量發
展。相較傳統 U-DIMM，R-DIMM 對訊號完整性及可靠度要求較高，
連接器製程與品質門檻亦隨之提升，有利高單價產品需求成長。優
群持續拓展 Meta 與 Google 等 CSP 客戶訂單，2026 年 R-DIMM 
累計出貨量已超越 2025 年全年，且高單價 R-DIMM 占比持續提
升，有助優化產品組合。其中，Google 伺服器新案預計於 1Q27 在
越南廠量產，除帶動 Long-DIMM 出貨成長，亦有助提升越南廠稼
動率並改善規模經濟，研究部預估 2027 年 Long-DIMM 營收成長 
30.9% YoY。網通方面，優群持續拓展既有筆電與伺服器以外的通
訊設備應用，新產品已於 3Q26 開始量產，預期 2027 年隨客戶訂
單增加及新應用拓展，出貨規模持續擴大。搭配 CAMM2、CCBB 等
新產品貢獻，研究部預估 2027 年 Others 營收成長 33.8% YoY，
成為營運結構轉型的重要成長來源。相較之下，傳統筆電 SO-DIMM 
營收預估衰退 2.4% YoY，顯示營運成長動能逐步由筆電轉向伺服
器與網通應用，有助降低對筆電景氣的依賴。

圖一、優群產品營收比重（2024～2027F）

資料來源：優群，統一投顧預估

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. 記憶體模組化與可維修設計趨勢，帶動高單價產品滲透 
隨筆電朝輕薄化、高速傳輸及可維修設計發展，連接器規格持續升
級，帶動高單價產品導入。記憶體方面，傳統 SO-DIMM 受限於模
組高度與訊號傳輸需求，部分高階筆電轉向焊接式 LPDDR 設計，
壓抑傳統連接器需求；CAMM2 則兼具低矮化、高速傳輸及模組可更
換等優勢，提供新一代記憶體連接方案。優群 CAMM2 目前月出貨
約 5～10 萬顆，HP 約 10 款高階機種正進行認證，預計 1H27 量
產，隨模組供應改善及新機種導入，可望擴大出貨規模。Type-C 方
面，隨 USB-C 成為筆電充電與資料傳輸的主要介面，接口維修成
本逐漸受到品牌重視。優群推出可獨立拆卸的兩件式 Type-C，無
須因接口損壞而更換整片主機板，單價約為傳統產品的兩倍，目前
已導入聯想機種，HP 預計於 2027 年加入。此外，Metal Bar 微
型沖壓件持續由筆電拓展至記憶體及 SiP 模組，憑藉精密沖壓與
微型化製程能力，擴大高毛利產品應用。研究部預估 2027 年 
Metal Bar 營收成長約 18% YoY，隨高單價新產品滲透率提升，有
助優化產品組合並支撐毛利率回升。

5. 筆電需求修正超出預期，下修 2026 年獲利，2027 年重返成
長 
考量筆電需求疲弱、客戶持續調整出貨，加上稼動率下降及原物料
成本上漲壓抑獲利，研究部下修優群 2026 年營收預估至 37.50 
億元（−9.6% YoY，原估 40.11 億元），其中 SO-DIMM 與 M.2 營
收分別衰退 29% 及 17% YoY，為主要拖累；毛利率由原估 47.9% 
下修至 47.0%，稅後淨利預估 9.03 億元（−21.0% YoY），稅後 
EPS 由 11.31 元下修至 10.06 元。 
展望 2027 年，隨 CSP 伺服器新案放量、網通業務擴張及高單價
新產品持續貢獻，研究部預估 Long-DIMM、Metal Bar 及其他產品
營收分別成長約 31%、18% 及 34% YoY，帶動全年營收回升至 
41.12 億元（+9.7% YoY，原估 46.67 億元）。隨營收規模擴大、
稼動率提升及產品組合改善，毛利率預估回升至 47.6%，稅後淨利 
10.54 億元（+16.8% YoY），稅後 EPS 11.83 元（+16.8% YoY，
原估 14.30 元）。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

6. 短期利空已逐步反映，低評價與高殖利率浮現投資價值 
考量筆電需求疲弱及短期獲利下修，優群股價已大幅修正，現價對
應 2027 年本益比僅 8.8 倍，低於歷史 10～20 倍區間下緣，反
映短期營運利空已逐步消化。此外，優群近五年現金配息率約 78%
～79%，以研究部預估 2026 年 EPS 10.06 元推算，現金股利約 
7.9 元，按 11/18 收盤價 104.5 元計算，預估殖利率約 7.6%，
提供下檔支撐。展望 2027 年，隨 CSP 伺服器與網通新業務放量，
營收及獲利可望重返成長，帶動評價修復。綜合考量短期利空已逐
步反映、獲利成長動能回升及殖利率支撐，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 
為評價基礎，給予 12 倍本益比，目標價 140 元，投資評等由中
立調升至買進。

料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之

1 
4540 全球傳動(TBI MOTION)

報告日期：2026/11/19 研 究 員：洪 國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 財報受惠產品組合優化，呆帳回沖，業外匯兌利益挹注，
單季 EPS 0.79 元，優於先前預期 0.44 元。 
 蘇州新廠將於 1Q~2Q27 正式投產，將有助舒緩台灣樹林廠產
能吃緊情況。 
 桃園大溪廠己於 4Q26 正式處分，預計 1Q27 交割完成，處分
利益 1.1 億元，貢獻全球傳動 EPS 0.95 元。 
一. 公告 3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.79 元 
在客戶訂單回溫，全球傳動 3Q26 營收金額 9.46 億元，
YoY+40.27%，QoQ+13.96%，營業利益 0.81 億元，較 3Q25 由
虧轉盈，QoQ+63.74%，稅後盈餘 0.92 億 元，較 3Q25 由虧轉盈，
QoQ+80.7%，稅後 EPS 0.79 元。1Q~3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.04 元，
優於去年同期-1.08 元。 
二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 0.56 元 
全球 10/2026 營收金額 2.88 億元，YoY+11.5%，MoM-11.14%。
全球傳動 4Q26 將致力優化產品組合為目標，預估全球傳動 4Q26
營收金額 9.36 億元，YoY+21.5%，QoQ-1.05%，預估營業利益
0.59 億元，較 4Q25 由虧轉盈，QoQ-27.6%，預估稅後盈餘 0.64
億元，YoY+38.2%，QoQ-30.1%，預估稅後 EPS 0.56 元。 
三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 1.60 元 
全球傳動 2026 年傳動元件產品如行星滾柱螺桿與微型螺桿持續
切入機器人，半導體與自動化設備領域，產能利用率回溫，帶動
由虧轉盈。預估全球傳動 2026 年營收金額 35 億元，YoY+36.8%，
預估稅後盈餘 1.84 億元，由虧轉盈，預估稅後 EPS 1.60 元。 
四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 3.30 元 
全球傳動 2027 年在中國大陸持續大力發展機器人領域，將有助全
球行星滾柱螺桿與微型螺桿產品切入，另外大溪廠處分利益 1Q27
認列，有助全球傳動 2027 年營運表現。預估全球傳動 2027 年營
收金額 40 億元，YoY+14.3% ，預估稅後盈餘 3.80 億元，
YoY+105.97%，預估稅後 EPS 3.30 元。 
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 66 元 
觀察機器人執行機構件包括旋轉執行器，線性執行器，末端執行
器，全球傳動己小量成功打入線性執行器與靈巧手廠商供應鏈，
將有助評價提升，故投資建議買進，目標價 66 元。(28X 2027 年
扣除大溪廠土地後稅後 EPS=20X 2027 年稅後 EPS ) 
投資評等：買進

2

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
全球傳動科技(TBI MOTION)成立於 2010 年，資 本 額 11.52 億元，
並於 2018 年掛牌上市，全球傳動為台灣傳動元件專業製造廠，TBI 
MOTION 掌握關鍵核心技術，專注創新研發與設計，擁有多項產
品設計專利並且通過 ISO9001 認證，經銷營業據點分佈全球，廣
獲各國客戶的肯定。TBI MOTION 產品線齊全、MIT 台灣製造生
產，主要產品：滾珠螺桿、線性滑軌、滾珠花鍵、旋轉式滾珠螺
桿/花鍵、單軸機器人、直線軸承、聯軸器、螺桿支撐座..等。 產
品的應用範圍非常廣泛，實際應用於自動化產業、半導設備、產
業機械、工具機產業、綠能產業與機器人相關產業。全球傳動營
收地區方面，中國大陸 74~82%、台灣地區 7~10%、亞洲地區
6~10%、歐洲地區 3~5%、美洲地區 1%。

(單軸機器人)。

圖二、全球傳動產品

資料來源：公司&宏遠投顧彙整

預估 2027 年滾珠螺桿營收金額 21.00 億元，YoY+12.9%

滾珠螺桿主要功用在於藉由鋼珠於螺桿與螺帽之間進行運動，由
於滾珠螺桿在頻繁返復運轉時，鋼珠間會產生摩擦熱度導致出現
氧化或脫碳現象而縮短使用壽命，全球傳動滾珠螺桿以轉造級螺
桿為主，所提供標準規格為 6mm~80mm，亦可提供研磨級螺桿規
格為 4mm~160mm，螺帽部分則以循環方式區分內循環、外循環、
端蓋式循環。滾珠螺桿 2025 年營收金額 14.73 億元，
YoY+0.00%，在中國大陸製造業景氣低迷，導致客戶訂單量維持
在低檔水準。

滾珠螺桿 1Q~3Q26 營收金額 13.59 億元，YoY+33.27%。預 期 2026
年，全球傳動己切入人形機器人的關鍵零組件，包括線型執行器
與靈巧手。觀察一台人型機器人需要用到 14 個線型執行器，一個
線型執行器需要一台行星滾柱螺桿；靈巧手方面，一台人型機器
人需要兩隻靈巧手，每隻靈巧手需要 15 支微型滾珠螺桿。上述都
己分別打入台灣與中國大陸機器人相關供應鏈，預期 2026 年開始
小量出貨。另外半導體則切入後段封裝設備廠，預期機器人及半
導體可望為營運成長動能。宏遠預估 2026 年滾珠螺桿相關產品營
收金額 18.60 億元，YoY+2633%。

預期 2027 年，在滾珠螺桿在自動化設備與機器人市場規模持續擴
大帶動下，另外微小型研磨螺桿己打入 CPO 設備模組供應鏈，將
有助滾珠螺桿出貨持續成長，宏遠預估 2027 年滾珠螺桿相關產品
營收金額 21.00 億元，YoY+12.9%。

預估 2027 年線性滑軌營收金額 16.35 億元，YoY+14.3%

線性滑軌屬滾動導引裝置，主要藉由鋼珠於滑塊與滑軌之間作無
限滾動循環，以維持負載平台順沿滑軌進行高精度線性運動，其
中技術層次要求須將線性滑軌摩擦係數降至傳統滑動導引的
1/50，以及滑塊與滑軌間末制單元設計須能同時承受各方向負
荷。全球傳動標準型線性滑軌尺吋涵蓋 15mm~65mm，另可提供
7mm~15mm 之微小型滑軌。線性滑軌 2025 年營收金額 9.41 億
元，YoY+35.2%。

年，全球傳動在中國自動化設備、電動車及鋰電池等新能源應用

預估 2027 年滾珠花鍵與其他營收金額 2.65 億元，YoY+26.2%

滾珠花鍵特色相較於滾珠花鍵可大負荷容量、旋轉方向零間隙、
高度靈敏性、高剛性與裝配簡單等。其他產品主要以單軸機器人
為主，單軸機器人採用線軌與螺桿的優點，將螺帽與滑塊設計成
一體式機構，並搭配高剛性的 U 型軌最佳化斷面，可達到最佳的
空間節省及大幅度減少組裝的時間，確保其高剛性、高精度要求。
滾珠花鍵與其他產品 2025 年營收金額 1.46 億元，YoY+16.1%。

在自動化智慧工廠趨勢明確，全球將持續開發新產品，加上產能
擴充帶動，將有助滾珠花鍵與單軸機器人恢復成長動能，預估滾
珠花鍵與其他 2026 年營收金額 2.1 億元，YoY+44.03%。

公司拜訪快報

達興材料(5234)：半導體材料轉型效益逐步顯現 
建議：維持買進

一、公司積極朝半導體特化廠轉
型 
 達興材料成立於 2006 年，過去為國內最大面板化學品供應商，近年則積極朝半
導體材料發展。營運總部及研發中心位於台中中科，而台中中科、中港各有一座
先進材料製造中心；桃園、蘇州、深圳設有業務據點。9M26 產品組合中，顯示
器材料佔營收 75%、半導體材料 23%，關鍵原材料則為 2.0%。銷售區域來看，
台灣、中國分別佔營收 67%及 32%，其他國家約 1%。

二、4Q26 獲利率重返上升軌道  受惠半導體材料營運增長，3Q26 營收季增 7.8%至 13.82 億元，但顯示器材料
來料異常而認列跌價損失 3,000 萬元，導致毛利率略為滑落至 41.3%，單季 EPS
為 2.29 元(+2.7%QoQ, +17.0%YoY)；累計前三季 EPS 為 6.73 元(+26.1%YoY)。
展望 4Q26，半導體彌補顯示器營收缺口，管理層預期營收持平至小幅季增，在
原料成本漲勢趨緩且無跌價損失下，毛利率將重回上升趨勢，預估單季 EPS 為
2.63 元(+15.1%QoQ, +19.5%YoY)。公司近期公告 Oct-26 自結獲利，營益率較
3Q26 的 19.9%提高至 21.9%。

三、半導體材料挹注中長期營運
動能 
 隨新產能持續投入且生產效率提高，累計前三季半導體營收成長 60.3%至 8.96
億元，其中，先進製程與先進封裝應用貢獻各半。此外，3Q26 半導體材料營收
比重達 27.1%，大幅年增 8.1 個百分點，管理層預期 4Q26 佔比將進一步擴大
至 30%左右，目標在 2030 年比重達到 50%。目前半導體材料量產品項為 13
支，包括 7 支自製產品、6 支與國際大廠策略合作生產，2H26 有 5 支產品上線
測試，而驗證中品項則有 14 支。目前中港二廠 1 樓的 10 條線已滿載，2027 年
將投入 2 樓的 10 條線，帶動營收維持雙位數增幅。公司為支應客戶對半導體材
料的長期需求，投入 22 億元興建橋科新廠，預計 2028 年完工、2029 年開始貢
獻。

四、顯示器藉產品組合維持獲利
表現 
 2027 年台灣顯示器客戶有做產能削減規劃，但中國客戶還是有新產品需求。另
一方面，公司新設立江蘇子公司，主要配合中國顯示器客戶的材料在地化需求，
也透過該公司持續開拓顯示器的客源。中長期營運方向也包括優化生產製程、提
高原料自製比例，保持 PS & Cu 蝕刻液優勢並提升市佔率，也同步擴大液晶配
向膜 PSA PI 導入新量產廠區。

五、維持買進理由  受惠半導體材料動能持續成長，加上顯示器材料產品組合優化，預估 2026、2027
年 EPS 為 9.36 及 11.97 元。隨中港廠完成生產空間擴充，最大產能由 24 條擴
增至 40 條線且生產效率提升，而橋頭新廠則預計 1Q27 動工、2028 年完工，

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

삼성전자 (005930)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

### Page 8

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q 부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520 억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1 조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV 에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.
Investment Implications
Oil market conditions remain a swing
factor for the outlook. A shift lower in
oil risk premia and improvement on
the refining front would go a long way

toward easing inflation and interest
rates while bolstering risk-asset
appetite. A gradual increase in Fixed
Income portfolio duration continues to
make sense, in our view.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings have generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than two-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.”2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market.2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of investment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractive in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market moves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting government
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff
Source: Chief Investment Office. Data as of October 22, 2026.
* “Pop the Champagne,” Portfolio Insights, Chief Investment Office, August 2026.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.
Nonfinancial assets, such as closely held businesses, real estate, fine art, oil, gas and mineral properties, and timber, farm and ranch land, are complex in nature and involve risks including total

Document 345: FX Daily_ Higher US real rates dominate

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Higher US real rates dominate.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

US short-dated real interest rates are pushing back to their
highest levels since 2023 as markets get the message that the
Fed is ready to apply the brakes. These high real rates are
sucking funds out of asset classes like precious metals and are
triggering a broader fallout across emerging market currencies.
There is no reason to ﬁght this trend near term

Chris Turner, Francesco Pesole and Frantisek Taborsky
Higher US real rates are sucking funds into the dollar
USD: Two-year real swap rates are now
2.4%
The market has got the message. We understand the Fed needs to
take rates higher to get inﬂation back to target. And we are now
seeing US two-year real rates (swap rates less the zero-coupon
inﬂation swap) pushing above 2.40%. The last time rates were at
this level was in 2023. Higher real rates are especially negative for
zero-yielding precious metals and one of the reasons why gold and
silver and linked currencies like the South African rand and Thai
baht are under pressure.

### Page 2

On the Fed, we have seen further comments from Chris Waller
alongside the release of the October FOMC minutes over the last 24
hours. Waller has now turned ultra-hawkish and is pushing for
more than a reversal of the 75bp 'insurance' rate cuts seen last
year. However, he may be out on his own here. According to the
FOMC minutes, only a 'couple' of Fed voters favoured a faster pace
of tightening at the October meeting and the market now looks
quite settled on pricing 25bp hikes in December, January and next

summer. When could these views be challenged? Perhaps not until
energy-softened November CPI data is released on 10 December.
This all seems a very positive story for the dollar then. There has
also been speculation that foreign interest in Anthropic's
blockbuster IPO has seen some demand for the dollar this week. If
that is the case, that dollar demand could wane into tomorrow
once IPO allocations are settled. Yet there are good reasons for the
dollar to be at current levels, and DXY advancing to the 104.60 area

remains our bias for now.
For today, all eyes will be on how Anthropic (ANT) trades on its ﬁrst
day, while low initial jobless claims should point to a solid US jobs
market.
Chris Turner
EUR: Macro resilience, but swamped by
the dollar
EUR/USD is consolidating under 1.10 as the strong dollar dominates
across the board. EUR/USD price action masks ongoing resilience on
the macro side, where business conﬁdence remains relatively high
and better-than-expected eurozone growth rates this year are

projected to continue into 2027. As above, it is hard to see the
strong dollar trend turning before mid-December, and we think
EUR/USD still risks a drop to the 1.0880 area.
A light data calendar today just sees the release of September
eurozone current account data. High energy prices in September
may have weighed further on the surplus, although Germany

Chris Turner
ZAR: SARB to leave rates on hold today
The South African Reserve Bank meets to set interest rates today.

Having hiked interest rates to 7.25% in September, no one is
expecting a further hike today. However, money markets do price
another 25bp hike in early 2027. Unlike Banxico, the SARB has been
responsive to the inﬂation shock and we see much greater scope
for the rand to bounce back than Mexico's peso. See a more in-
depth discussion on the rand's prospects in our 2027 FX Outlook.
Chris Turner
MXN: Lack of rate protection comes
home to roost
At one point, USD/MXN was nearly 3% higher on the day as large

parts of the emerging market complex succumbed to the strong
dollar. We heavily discussed the risk of peso underperformance in
our 2027 Latam FX Outlook, given that Banxico had cut the policy
rate spread protection over the Fed to very slim levels. We see no
reason for USD/MXN to turn lower until the broader dollar trend
turns. And, ultimately, that may not be until the second quarter of
next year, when US inﬂation is substantially falling.
Chris Turner

Document 431: US Economics Analyst_ 2027 US Outlook_ The End of the Inflation Shocks

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Economics Analyst_ 2027 US Outlook_ The End of the Inflation Shocks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 20
Status: ok

### Page 1

The main economic surprise of 2026, the energy shock from the Iran war, has not n
prevented the US economy from growing at a healthy pace but has reversed 
progress on inflation.  The key drivers of growth and inflation in 2026—the war, 
tax cuts, tariffs, and AI price pressures—should all have less impact next year.  
We expect this to leave 2027 GDP growth at an above-consensus 2.4% and the 
labor market stable, while allowing core PCE inflation to fall substantially to 2.4%. 
Growth should follow the same pattern in 2027 as in 2026: solid consumption n
growth and strong business investment growth offset by drags from housing and 
trade.  Consumption should grow a bit over 2%, driven mainly by firmer and more 
evenly balanced real income growth as inflation moderates and the boosts from 
last year’s tax cuts and wealth effects diminish.  AI drives half of our 8% forecast 
for business investment growth but contributes only modestly to measured GDP 
growth because much of the equipment is imported or undercounted in the GDP 
statistics.  Housing investment will likely contract again as poor affordability 
continues to depress demand. 
Near-potential growth should keep the labor market stable in 2027.  This year n
began with fears of a looming AI-driven layoff wave, but the layoff rate instead 
remained historically low, and the labor market settled into a slightly softer 
low-hire, low-fire form of full employment where wage growth has stabilized at a 
subdued pace of about 3.5%.  We expect this equilibrium to last for another year 
before AI displacement begins to have a larger impact in subsequent years. 
While the near-term inflation outlook remains uncertain, we are more confident n
that inflation will fall substantially by late 2027 as the effects of tariffs, the Iran 
war energy shock, and AI price pressures diminish.  We see these three forces as 
the key to the outlook because the economy is not overheated at a 
macroeconomic level or even at an industry level outside of pockets tied to the 
AI boom, and high inflation does not appear to have become entrenched in 
expectations.  As a result, inflation should moderate as long as these forces 
subside and no new shocks emerge. 
The Fed is likely to remain firmly focused on inflation in 2027.  We forecast two n
more 25bp rate hikes in December 2026 and March 2027 to a peak of 
4.25-4.5%, followed by gradual cuts back to 3.25-3.5% starting in November, the 
final meeting of 2027, once inflation has declined meaningfully.  The FOMC will 
also discuss the conclusions of the task forces next year, but we expect any 
David Mericle 
+1(212)357-2619 | 
david.mericle@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
15 November 2026 | 11:59PM EST

### Page 2

institutional changes to be limited.  In particular, a large reduction in the size of the
Fed’s balance sheet is unlikely, though a shift in the composition of its assets is
possible.
The most obvious risk to our forecast of sturdy growth and a long-awaited decline in n
inflation is that the inflation shocks of the last two years, which we and most
forecasters did not anticipate, could last longer, or new shocks could arise.  We also
see downside risks to growth from the war, a longer period of higher interest rates,

or earlier and more rapid job displacement from AI.  But our best guess is that there
will be fewer big policy-driven macroeconomic shocks in 2027.
15 November 2026   2
Goldman Sachs US Economics Analyst

### Page 3

2027 US Outlook: The End of the Inflation Shocks

The main economic surprise of 2026, the energy shock from the Iran war, has not 
prevented the US economy from growing at a healthy pace.  We expect GDP growth of 
2.5% in 2026 Q4/Q4, in line with our forecast from our 2026 Outlook but above both 
consensus expectations at the time and our forecast after the war began.  In contrast, 
the combination of the energy shock and price pressures sparked by the AI investment 
boom are likely to leave core PCE inflation at 3.2% on a Q4/Q4 basis, a disappointment 
to the more optimistic expectation for a large decline that we and consensus held at the 
start of the year.

The key drivers of growth and inflation in 2026—the war, tax cuts, tariffs, and AI price 
pressures—should all have less incremental impact in 2027.  We expect this to leave 
2027 Q4/Q4 GDP growth at 2.4% (above consensus at 2.2%) and the labor market 
stable, while allowing core PCE inflation to fall to 2.4% (in line with consensus).

### Page 4

The Growth Outlook: Solid Consumption, Strong Business Investment, 
and Weak Housing Balance Out to Near-Potential GDP Growth 
Growth should follow the same pattern in 2027 as in 2026: solid consumption growth 
and strong business investment growth offset by drags from housing and trade.  We 
expect this to balance out to overall GDP growth that is a hair above our 2.3% estimate 
of potential growth.  Relative to consensus, our growth forecast is a touch higher on 
consumer spending, much higher on business investment, and notably weaker on 
housing and net exports.

### Page 5

The resilience of the US economy this year in the face of the energy shock has been 
particularly impressive in the case of consumer spending.  Despite a boost to headline 
inflation that has left real wage growth close to zero this year, consumer spending has 
grown at a strong pace, supported by tax refunds, a positive wealth effect from the 
stock market, and a modest decline in the saving rate. 
Our consumer outlook for 2027 is less complicated: we expect 2.2% growth in consumer 
spending driven mainly by stronger real labor income growth as inflation moderates, 
with special factors playing a more limited role.  We do not expect a meaningful change 
in the saving rate, which is likely close to 5% after accounting for revised spending and 
income data that are incorporated with a lag, putting it roughly in line with what one 
would expect at a time when household wealth is high and layoff risk is low. 
Both real income and consumer spending should grow more evenly across the income 
distribution in 2027.  While we noted last year that real income was actually more in line 
with pre-pandemic trends across income quintiles than the K-shaped narrative 
suggested, in 2026 income growth has been weaker for low-income families, due in part 
to fiscal policy changes and the unequal burden of the energy shock.  Income growth 
should even out in 2027, with a boost at the bottom from lower inflation, cost-of-living 
adjustments to government transfer payments, and the likely reversal of recent cuts to 
social spending.

AI will remain the dominant driver of business investment growth in 2027.  We estimate 
that spending on AI-related hardware and intellectual property will exceed $900bn 
annualized by end-2026 (a $550bn increase from 2025) and $1.4tn by end-2027 (a 
further $500bn increase), roughly 4% of GDP. 
The midterm election results should pose only modest risks to AI investment in 2027.  
We expect newly elected Democratic governors in states that account for over a third of 
planned US data center capacity to impose moratoria on new data centers.  But these 
are unlikely to be long-lived and should only affect projects not yet permitted, leaving 
the vast majority of planned 2027 construction unaffected.
